- **shannon**: Use when reasoning about information entropy — Shannon's H = −Σ pᵢ·log₂(pᵢ) bits, the expected surprisal of a distribution: the average bits per symbol and the irreducible limit of lossless compression. It is the information twin of thermodynamic entropy (same −Σp ln p, different constant) — the bits a message carries and the bits a tamper must reproduce. - **SHAP**: SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) is a unified, game-theoretic framework for local feature attribution. Treating a model's input features as players in a coalition game, SHAP assigns each feature its Shapley value — the average marginal contribution of that feature to the prediction across all possible feature subsets. Shapley values are the unique attributions satisfying local accuracy, missingness, and consistency, so the prediction decomposes additively into a baseline plus one Shapley value per feature, giving a per-prediction explanation that is theoretically grounded and model-agnost… ([zjunlp/Mechanist](https://github.com/zjunlp/Mechanist/tree/HEAD/skills/mechanism-skills/SHAP) · ★75)
- **shap**: SHAP is a unified approach to explain machine learning model outputs using Shapley values from cooperative game theory.
